Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models enable cross-lingual transfer of knowledge across a wide range of NLP tasks, but their evaluation remains highly uneven across high-, mid- and low-resource languages. In this paper, we propose a two-dimensional framework, specifically tailored for analyzing multilingual embedding benchmarks under dataset scarcity, and apply it on the Slavic-language subset of the MTEB benchmark. The framework distinguishes between task-specific and cross-task evaluation, while jointly analyzing three complementary aspects: (1) ranking robustness, (2) model consistency, and (3) evidence strength. At the task-specific level, we evaluate the stability of model rankings under changes in ranking methodology and benchmark dataset composition. At the cross-task level, we assess the ability of models to generalize across diverse tasks within a language. To quantify the reliability of benchmark conclusions, we introduce an Evidence Strength Score that accounts for dataset availability, diversity, and robustness assessability. Our analysis reveals severe benchmark sparsity, with many Slavic language-task pairs relying on a single dataset or highly correlated benchmark collections, limiting the ability to draw robust conclusions. The cross-task analysis reveals a small group of highly transferable models, most notably llama-embed-nemotron-8b, multilingual-e5-large-instruct, and Qwen3-Embedding variants, that consistently perform well across Slavic languages and tasks. Overall, the results demonstrate that benchmark rankings and robustness conclusions must be interpreted jointly with certain notation of their evidence strength and highlight benchmark scarcity as a major obstacle to trustworthy multilingual evaluation.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Information Seeking: Severity-Aware Question Supervision for Proactive Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24521)
*Chenxuan Li,Xinrong Chen,Luyan Zhang,Peidong Jia,Zhongyu Zhao,Xuecheng Shang,Peixing Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Expected-Severity-Risk (ESR)目标函数，使主动医疗对话中的问题选择不仅考虑信息增益，还考虑不同诊断错误后果的严重性差异。ESR通过训练集统计量对候选问题打分，并蒸馏为前缀-only语言策略。在DDxPlus上，相比匹配的不确定性监督，高严重性诊断遗漏降低29.5%，诊断准确率从.9123提升到.9320。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息获取方法只优先选择最能降低诊断不确定性的问题，忽略了医疗诊断中不同错误后果差异巨大的特性——漏掉严重疾病比在低风险备选项间减少不确定性重要得多。问题选择应同时考虑新证据的信息量和它对下游诊断决策的预期影响。

Method: 提出ESR (Expected-Severity-Risk)监督目标：根据每个候选问题对严重性感知的终端风险（severity-aware terminal risk）的预期降低来评估其价值。由于问题在答案观测前选择，ESR使用仅训练集的总体统计对可能答案进行边缘化。随后将ESR排序蒸馏为prefix-only语言策略，使部署时无需教师端计算。

Result: 在DDxPlus上的Qwen3-4B三个训练种子实验中：ESR监督将平均高严重性诊断遗漏从.0645降到.0455（-29.5%），平均诊断准确率从.9123提升到.9320，且每对话仅增加0.14个问题。固定预算分析表明两个目标在控制问题数量时行为仍不同；匹配的预期0/1风险对照表明严重性感知加权改进高严重性错误轮廓优于通用的决策感知监督。

Conclusion: 结果支持主动医疗对话应超越不确定性缩减，转向后果感知（consequence-aware）的证据获取。ESR展示了将诊断错误的后果不对称性纳入问题选择流程的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Proactive medical dialogue requires an agent to decide what to ask from incomplete patient information. Existing information-seeking approaches commonly prioritize questions that most reduce diagnostic uncertainty. While effective for acquiring informative evidence, this criterion overlooks an important property of medical diagnosis: different diagnostic errors can carry substantially different consequences. Missing a severe condition may matter more than reducing uncertainty among less consequential alternatives. Question acquisition should therefore consider not only how informative new evidence is, but also how it is expected to affect the downstream diagnostic decision. To this end, we propose Expected-Severity-Risk (ESR), a consequence-aware question-supervision objective that values each candidate by its expected reduction in severity-aware terminal risk. Because questions must be selected before their answers are observed, ESR marginalizes over possible answers using train-only population statistics. Its rankings are then distilled into a prefix-only language policy, so next-question selection requires no teacher-side computation at deployment. Across three Qwen3-4B training seeds on DDxPlus, matched ESR supervision reduces mean high-severity diagnostic miss from .0645 to .0455 (-29.5%) and improves mean diagnostic accuracy from .9123 to .9320 while requiring only 0.14 additional questions per dialogue. Fixed-budget analyses show that the two objectives remain behaviorally distinct when question count is controlled, while a matched expected-0/1-risk control shows that severity-aware weighting improves the high-severity error profile beyond generic decision-aware supervision. These results support moving proactive medical dialogue beyond uncertainty reduction toward consequence-aware evidence acquisition.

</details>


### [134] [Is Discrete Difficulty Sufficient? Leveraging Continuous Difficulty for Efficient Self-Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24590)
*Sihyeong Yeom,Geon Park,Geunyeong Jeong,Taewoong Yoon,Jaewook Lee,Harksoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Flexible Self-Consistency (FSC)，通过预训练探针预测输出熵作为连续难度信号，动态调整推理路径数量，在保持与SC相当准确率的同时节省高达76%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-Consistency虽在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但生成多条推理路径导致token消耗过大。现有改进方法将难度分为少数固定级别，无法充分捕捉推理复杂度的连续变化特性，因此需要一种能连续估计难度并动态分配采样预算的方法。

Method: 提出FSC框架，使用预训练探针预测输入问题的输出熵，将熵作为模型不确定性的连续指标，据此灵活控制生成的推理路径数量（采样预算）。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，FSC在保持与标准SC相近准确率的同时，实现了最高76%的token节省。

Conclusion: FSC通过连续的难度估计替代固定分级，能够更精准地按需分配计算资源，在准确率和效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了基于熵的连续自适应采样策略的有效性。

Abstract: Self-Consistency (SC) is a decoding strategy that samples diverse reasoning paths and selects the most consistent answer, demonstrating strong performance on complex reasoning problems. However, the excessive token consumption incurred by generating multiple reasoning paths has been identified as a major limitation of SC. To improve computational efficiency, several studies have proposed strategies that adjust the number of reasoning paths or allocate resources differentially according to problem difficulty. Nevertheless, most existing methods categorize difficulty into a few fixed levels, failing to fully capture the continuously varying nature of reasoning complexity. In this work, we propose Flexible Self-Consistency (FSC), which estimates problem difficulty as a continuous signal and dynamically adjusts the number of generated reasoning paths accordingly. FSC predicts the output entropy of an input question using a pre-trained probe and leverages it as an indicator of model uncertainty to flexibly control the sampling budget. Experimental results show that, across various models and benchmarks, FSC maintains accuracy comparable to SC while achieving token savings of up to 76%.

</details>


### [135] [Quantization Effects on Bangla Language Understanding in Large Language Models: A Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24615)
*Ismail Hossain,Nafi Ullah Shafin,Mohammad Abdullah Al Mumin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对孟加拉语（形态复杂、低资源语言）的大语言模型进行受控的后训练量化对比研究。作者评估了三个模型家族（Qwen-2.5-7B、LLaMA-3.1-8B、GPT-OSS-20B）在全精度和三种量化格式（GPTQ-Int8、GPTQ-Q8、GGUF-W8A16）下在五个孟加拉语NLU基准上的零样本表现。结果显示模型家族对量化的响应差异显著：GPT-OSS在GGUF-W8A16下推理密集任务精度损失高达57.35%，而Qwen和LLaMA在GPTQ下保持稳定甚至偶有超越全精度，BoolQ-BN理解任务在所有条件下保持稳定。结论是量化对孟加拉语部署可行，但架构和量化方法的选择比比特宽度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化（PTQ）常被用于降低LLM内存占用和加速推理，尤其适合端侧部署，但现有研究几乎全部基于英文基准。孟加拉语作为形态复杂且资源稀缺的语言，其量化效果是否与英文一致完全未知。这一知识空白可能导致实践者在孟加拉语设备端部署时做出错误选择，因此需要系统性地填补。

Method: 采用受控对比实验设计：选择三个模型家族（Qwen-2.5-7B、LLaMA-3.1-8B、GPT-OSS-20B），在三种量化格式（GPTQ-Int8、GPTQ-Q8、GGUF-W8A16）与全精度之间进行零样本评估。基准覆盖五个孟加拉语NLU任务：Bangla MMLU、CommonsenseQA-BN、OpenBookQA-BN、PIQA-BN（推理/常识类）和BoolQ-BN（理解类），使用lm-evaluation-harness工具统一评测。

Result: 三个模型家族对量化的响应不同：GPT-OSS在GGUF-W8A16格式下推理密集任务精度损失高达57.35%；Qwen和LLaMA在GPTQ格式下表现稳定，个别情况下量化版本甚至优于全精度；BoolQ-BN理解任务在所有家族和格式下均保持稳定。证明架构与量化方法的组合效应强于比特宽度本身。

Conclusion: 量化可用于孟加拉语的低资源语言部署，但不应仅关注比特宽度。实践者需根据目标任务的推理/理解属性，结合具体模型架构选择量化格式——例如推理任务更适合配合稳定的架构使用GPTQ，而理解类任务对量化容忍度更高。研究为约束硬件上的模型选型提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lowers the memory footprint of Large Language Models (LLMs) and speeds up inference, which is why it is now common for on-device deployment. Most of what we know about its effects, however, comes from English benchmarks. It is not clear whether the same holds for morphologically complex, low-resource languages such as Bangla, and this gap is what we address here. We evaluate three model families---Qwen-2.5-7B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, and GPT-OSS-20B---in full precision and in three quantized formats (GPTQ-Int8, GPTQ-Q8, GGUF-W8A16) across five Bangla natural language understanding benchmarks (Bangla MMLU, CommonsenseQA-BN, OpenBookQA-BN, PIQA-BN, and BoolQ-BN), using zero-shot evaluation through lm-evaluation-harness. To our knowledge this is the first controlled comparison of quantization formats on Bangla NLU. The three families do not respond the same way: GPT-OSS loses up to 57.35% accuracy on reasoning-heavy tasks under GGUF-W8A16, while Qwen and LLaMA hold steady under GPTQ, and in a few cases the quantized version edges out the full-precision one. BoolQ-BN, a comprehension task, stays stable across all three families regardless of format. Taken together, these results suggest quantization can work well for Bangla deployment, but the choice of architecture and quantization method matters more than the bit width alone. We discuss what this means for practitioners choosing a model to run on constrained hardware.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Semantic Accuracy: Consequence-Aware Evaluation for Safety-Critical Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24621)
*Yujing Chang,Thinh Pham,Van-Phat Thai,Chunyao Ma,Yash Guleria,Pham Nhut Huy,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在安全关键场景（如空中交通管制）中的可靠性问题，指出现有语义评分指标会高估模型的实际运营可靠性，并提出了一种后果感知评估框架来更真实地衡量风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，标准语义指标（如F1）无法反映错误操作带来的不对称后果（如误读高度、漏掉执行条件、混淆呼号），需要后果感知的评估方式来测试模型的实际可靠性。

Method: 构建了一个以航空标准为依据、并由来自三个国家的40名空中交通管制员提供反馈的受控诊断性ATC基准测试框架，利用后果感知评估对8个语言模型进行评测，并对模型进行风险感知微调。

Result: 发现存在系统性的"语义-安全差距"：传统评分比后果感知评估给出的性能估计显著偏高，即使那些在标准指标下看似可靠的模型也存在此问题。风险感知微调虽能缩小差距但无法完全消除。

Conclusion: 在做出真实的安全关键部署声明之前，后果感知评估是标准NLP指标的必要补充。

Abstract: Can language models be trusted in safety- critical operations? In such settings, strong per- formance on semantic metrics does not guaran- tee operational reliability: a misread altitude, a dropped execution condition, or a confused call- sign may score well under standard F1 yet carry sharply asymmetric operational consequences. We study this problem in air traffic control (ATC), where controller-pilot communication demands near-zero error tolerance, and use consequence-aware evaluation to test whether semantic scores misstate operational reliabil- ity. The framework is instantiated in a con- trolled diagnostic ATC benchmark grounded in aviation standards and feedback from 40 air traffic controllers across three countries. Evaluating 8 models, we uncover a system- atic semantic-safety gap: conventional scores give substantially higher performance estimates than consequence-aware evaluation, even for models that appear reliable under standard met- rics. Risk-aware fine-tuning narrows but does not close this gap, showing that consequence- aware evaluation is a necessary complement to standard NLP metrics before any real safety- critical deployment claim

</details>


### [137] [Expectation, Backlash, Recovery, and Excitement: How Model Releases Shape Reddit Perceptions of Conversational AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24654)
*Vahid Rahimzadeh,Yury Zhauniarovich,Savvas Zannettou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对Reddit论坛的长期大规模分析，研究对话式AI系统（CAIS）用户感知如何随模型发布干预而动态变化。研究发现不同提供商（Anthropic、OpenAI、Grok、DeepSeek）表现出截然不同的感知模式，证明模型发布不仅是技术更新，更是重塑用户情感、期望和公共讨论的用户端干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CAIS用户感知研究大多将用户看法视为静态快照，忽视了模型发布、功能更新、安全干预和访问策略变动等动态因素对用户感知的持续影响。作者希望通过长期、大规模的数据分析来揭示这些干预如何动态塑造用户感知。

Method: 对Reddit讨论进行长期大规模分析，结合情感分类（sentiment classification）和主题概念分析（thematic concept analysis）两种方法，系统性地追踪不同AI提供商模型发布干预前后的用户感知变化。

Result: 不同提供商呈现截然不同的感知动态：Anthropic通过Claude Code和产品-模型匹配展现出最清晰的正向发布画像；OpenAI围绕GPT-5和GPT-5.1表现出反弹-恢复（backlash-and-recovery）的动态模式；Grok-3的感知受提供商身份和政治话语影响；DeepSeek-R1则兼具工程赞誉与对审查、访问和可靠性的担忧。

Conclusion: 模型发布不仅是技术更新，而是用户面向的干预（user-facing interventions），能够重塑用户的情感态度、期望和公共讨论格局。CAIS的感知本质上是动态的、对外部干预敏感的，研究需要超越静态快照的分析范式。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems (CAISes) continuously change through model releases, feature updates, safety interventions, and access-policy shifts, yet user perceptions are often studied as static snapshots. We conduct a long-term, large-scale analysis of Reddit discussions to examine how users perceive CAIS model release interventions across providers. By combining sentiment classification and thematic concept analysis, we show that CAIS perceptions are dynamic and intervention-sensitive. Anthropic exhibits the clearest positive release profile through Claude Code and product-model fit, OpenAI shows backlash-and-recovery dynamics around GPT-5 and GPT-5.1, Grok-3 is shaped by provider identity and political discourse, and DeepSeek-R1 combines engineering praise with concerns about censorship, access, and reliability. These findings show that model releases are not merely technical updates, but user-facing interventions that reshape sentiment, expectations, and public discussion.

</details>


### [138] [From local kernels to global form: modeling the emergence of musical content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24660)
*Francesco Vitucci,Michele Lorusso,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重叠滑动窗口的观测驱动估计机制，用于从单一符号序列推导局部转移核的轨迹，而非依赖外生形式划分。在德彪西《Syrinx》(1913)的273个逻辑音符事件上测试该机制，以A-B-A'解读为参照（而非ground truth）。在L=6时，两个参考边界在绝对音高和记谱时值两个维度均达到Jensen-Shannon最大值，但时值平台（64/267）远窄于音高平台（210/267）。音高在L=6的峰值因窗口几何上限（1/√(L-1)）及其宽平台而不足以作为独立证据；跨维度的对齐与边界敏感性一致，但宽平台使任一曲线不能单独作为唯一自动分段器。500次重合成实验在双维度量化了与源的偏离，并暴露了L=2时的精确复制退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非齐次马尔可夫模型通常依赖外生的形式划分来构建局部转移核，而本文希望探索一种纯数据驱动的、观测驱动的机制——仅从一个符号序列本身（通过重叠滑动窗口）推导局部过渡核的轨迹，从而评估其作为音乐分段工具的有效性。

Method: 采用重叠滑动窗口从一个符号序列中生成局部马尔可夫过渡核的轨迹，而非使用外生形式分区。以德彪西《Syrinx》的273个逻辑音符事件为测试数据，参照音乐学界常提出的A-B-A'解读作为参考而非ground truth。在绝对音高和记谱时值两个维度上分别构建Jensen-Shannon散度比较，并在L=6参数下分析两个参考边界的峰值表现，同时进行500次重合成实验量化与源序列的偏离。

Result: 在L=6时，A-B-A'的两个参考边界在音高和时值两个维度均达到Jensen-Shannon最大值；时值平台明显更窄（64/267）而音高平台宽得多（210/267）。由于滑动窗口比较的理论最大值为窗口几何所决定（最大进出过渡时为1/√(L-1)），音高在L=6的峰值及其宽平台本身不足以构成强证据。500次重合成实验暴露了L=2时的精确复制退化现象。

Conclusion: 观测驱动的滑动窗口估计机制在L=6时能在跨维度上对齐地检测到A-B-A'参考边界，展现出边界敏感性的潜力，但音高曲线的宽平台（涵盖78.7%的比较）使其不能单独作为唯一的自动分段器。该方法应被视为一种辅助性边界指示工具，而非独立的自上而下分段算法。L=2时的精确复制退化进一步确认了窗口长度的下限约束。

Abstract: Markov models are established tools for symbolic music, including non-homogeneous formulations. The narrower contribution examined here is an observation-driven estimation mechanism: overlapping sliding windows derive a trajectory of local transition kernels from one symbolic sequence rather than from an exogenous formal partition. We test this mechanism on 273 logical note events from Debussy's Syrinx (1913), using the often-proposed A-B-A' reading as a reference rather than ground truth. We apply the same validation to absolute-pitch and notated-duration kernels. At $L=6$, both reference boundaries attain the Jensen--Shannon maximum in both dimensions; the duration plateau is substantially narrower (64 of 267 comparisons) than the pitch plateau (210 of 267). Because the theoretical maximum for consecutive sliding-window comparisons is set by window geometry and equals $1/\sqrt{L-1}$ for maximal turnover of the entering/leaving transition, the pitch value at $L=6$ and its broad plateau are not, by themselves, strong evidence. Their cross-dimensional alignment is consistent with boundary sensitivity, while the broad plateaus preclude treating either curve alone as a unique automatic segmenter. Five-hundred-draw re-synthesis experiments quantify departure from the source in both dimensions and expose an exact-copy degeneracy at $L=2$.

</details>


### [139] [One Timeline, Many Renderings: A Wolfram Language Paclet for heterogeneous musical output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24683)
*Francesco Vitucci,Michele Lorusso,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Temporal System，一个Wolfram Language paclet，它通过一个不可变的类型化实体存储库（基于有理节拍时间线）和后端特定契约，统一编译算法作曲的多个输出（Csound乐谱、MusicXML 4.0记谱、OSC实时控制、排练节拍器），确保所有产物共享同一时间源而保持同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法作曲往往需要同时生成Csound乐谱、雕版记谱、实时控制信号和排练节拍等不同类型的产物，这些产物分别创作时容易出现时间线漂移（timeline drift）问题，导致各输出之间不同步。

Method: 采用单一不可变存储库存储类型化实体，基于有理节拍时间线构建；通过后端特定契约（backend-specific contracts）将存储编译为各类产物；合成、采样或赫兹等时间单位的转换延迟到渲染时进行；Csound音符使用外部.orc文件中的稳定命名乐器，曲线以k-rate信号声明在score p-fields上；节拍后端从相同的拍号和速度派生排练音频并复用Csound序列化器。

Result: 实现了Csound合成、beta MusicXML 4.0、OSC控制和排练节拍产物的同步输出，并描述了时序、语义和渲染契约层及其实际权衡，以及该专有创作环境在开源生态中的局限性。

Conclusion: Temporal System展示了通过统一时间存储模型和多后端契约编译，可以有效解决算法作曲多产物输出之间的同步问题，但当前受限于专有的Wolfram环境，且产物的完整发布有待paclet正式发布。

Abstract: One algorithmic composition may require a Csound score, engraved notation, real-time control, and a rehearsal click. Authored separately, their timelines drift. Temporal System is a Wolfram Language paclet that instead compiles one immutable store of typed entities on a rational beat timeline through backend-specific contracts. It emits Csound synthesis, beta MusicXML 4.0, OSC control, and click artifacts that remain synchronized because they share that store. Conversion to seconds, samples, or hertz occurs only at render time. Csound notes use stable named instruments in external .orc files; curves become k-rate signals declared against score p-fields. The click backend derives rehearsal audio from the same meter and tempo and reuses the Csound serializer. We describe the temporal, semantic, and rendering-contract layers, their practical trade-offs, and the limits of this proprietary authoring environment within an otherwise open-source ecosystem. The archived supplement exposes the reported outputs pending paclet release.

</details>


### [140] [The Annotation Bottleneck in Persian Text NLP: Persian as an Annotation-Scarce Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24698)
*MohammadHossein Mortazavi,Mostafa Salehi,Hadi Veisi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战"波斯语是低资源语言"的笼统说法，认为波斯语更应被精确描述为"标注稀缺"（annotation-scarce），且这是一个资源生态属性而非语言内在属性。作者综述了截至2026年7月的34个代表性波斯语文本资源，并通过三个定量交叉验证（网络可见性、语音资源轨迹、任务标注量归一化对比），表明波斯语的稀缺性体现为任务/领域覆盖不均、方案不兼容、获取与文档摩擦，而非整体标注量全球性短缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "低资源语言"这一标签将多种不同的资源短缺问题混为一谈，导致对波斯语NLP资源现状的误判，进而影响资源建设优先级和资金/研究方向的决策。作者试图提供更精确的诊断框架，以澄清波斯语资源生态的实际结构性短板。

Method: 综述34个代表性波斯语文本资源（截至2026年7月），并补充三个定量交叉检验：（1）用W3Techs和Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30测量波斯语网页占比（约0.9%和0.7039%），判断其网络可见性排名；（2）从FARSDAT到含数百上千小时语音语料库的语音资源演进回顾；（3）将波斯语与英语的任务级标注量按Common Crawl网页占比归一化，进行配对比较。

Result: 波斯语位居约前二十大网络内容语言；语音资源从FARSDAT延伸至数千小时的现代语料库；归一化后任务间差异悬殊：句法和新闻命名实体识别相对密集，而自然语言推理低于网页占比预期基线。并非波斯语在所有标注量上全球性匮乏。

Conclusion: 波斯语不应被简单归为"低资源"语言；其标注稀缺是结构性的，具体体现为任务与领域覆盖不均、标注方案不兼容、访问与文档摩擦显著，以及专业领域、偏好数据和标准伊朗波斯语之外变体的监督信号有限。这一精细化诊断有助于更精准地指导波斯语NLP资源建设。

Abstract: Persian (Farsi) is often described as a low-resource language in natural language processing, but that label collapses distinct shortages into a single category. This paper argues that Persian is more precisely described as annotation-scarce, provided that the term is understood as a property of its NLP resource ecology rather than an intrinsic property of the language. The review covers 34 representative Persian text resources available by July 2026 and adds three quantitative cross-checks. First, independent web measurements place Persian among roughly the twenty most visible content languages: W3Techs reports Persian on about 0.9% of websites with a known content language, while Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30 identifies Persian as the primary language of 0.7039% of HTML pages. Second, a selective speech review shows a long resource trajectory from FARSDAT to recent corpora containing hundreds or thousands of hours of speech. Third, a matched Persian-English comparison normalizes task-specific annotation volumes by relative Common Crawl web presence. The resulting ratios vary sharply: Persian syntax and news NER are comparatively dense, whereas natural-language inference falls below the web-proportional baseline. The evidence therefore does not support a simple claim that Persian is globally deficient in labeled volume. Instead, annotation scarcity is expressed through uneven task and domain coverage, incompatible schemes, access and documentation friction, and limited supervision for specialist domains, preference data, and varieties beyond standard Iranian Persian.

</details>


### [141] [Lost in Speech: Trilingual Spoken Hallucination Detection Across Audio and Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24707)
*Meruyert Aristombayeva,Jason S. Lucas,Chaewan Chun,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多语言口语幻觉检测基准，涵盖英语、俄语和哈萨克语共12,013条新闻样本，包含三种类型和三种严重程度的受控幻觉。同时补充了290条真实虚假新闻。实验表明基于文本转录的检测通常优于直接音频处理，且合成训练的检测器在真实虚假新闻上有较强迁移能力（macro-F1 0.82-0.88）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测研究主要集中在文本领域，口语幻觉检测（尤其是低资源语言）几乎未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立首个覆盖多语言的口语幻觉检测基准，并评估不同检测范式在口语场景下的表现。

Method: 构建包含12,013条英语、俄语和哈萨克语新闻样本的多语言口语幻觉基准，包含三种类型和三个严重级别的受控幻觉。样本包含原始文章及对齐的幻觉版本（文本和音频形式）。另外收集290条俄语（225）和哈萨克语（65）原生事实核查虚假新闻，通过翻译和TTS-ASR流水线处理。评估微调多语言编码器和零样本上下文学习中的多模态解码器，对比基于文本转录与直接音频处理的检测方式。

Result: 基于转录的检测普遍优于直接音频处理；强编码器的二分类任务性能下降与各语言ASR错误率相关。在真实虚假新闻上，合成训练的检测器迁移表现强劲（原始文本macro-F1为0.82-0.88）。俄语来源分析揭示了与真实性相关和模型依赖的机器风格信号，量化了合成幻觉基准中的关键混杂因素。

Conclusion: 确定了转录式检测在口语幻觉任务中的优势，验证了合成数据到真实数据的迁移能力，同时识别并量化了合成基准中机器风格这一重要混杂因素，为口语幻觉检测研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Arbitrary Polygon Oscillator: Generalizing Polygonal Synthesis to Arbitrary Shapes, Morphing, and Three-Dimensional Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24726)
*Antonio Argentieri,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的弧长引擎多边形成分合成系统：通过恒定弧长速度遍历任意顶点配置（规则/不规则/星形多边形）生成音频，支持不等顶点数多边形的混合插值平滑变形，并将范式扩展到三维凸多面体旋转切片产生的动态横截面波形，系统在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略和基于运行时Bézier切线的四点polyBLAMP抗混叠与自适应过采样相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的多边形成分合成系统限于恒角速度且仅适用于规则、参数化定义的多边形，只能在同一形状族内产生平滑的音色过渡，缺乏对任意/不规则几何、形态间变形以及维度扩展的通用性支持。

Method: 1) 将振荡器适配为接受外部缓冲区中的任意顶点配置，使规则、不规则或星形闭多边形均可作为波形发生器；2) 提出混合插值算法，实现顶点数不等多边形间的平滑变形，中间形态无参数化描述；3) 扩展至三维：凸多面体绕三轴旋转并被固定水平面切片，横截面产生由实体方向控制的连续可变多边形；4) 系统在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略避免逐采样重新计算；抗混叠结合四点polyBLAMP校正（由运行时Bézier切线推导）与自适应过采样。

Result: 系统在RNBO中成功运行，几何缓存策略避免了逐采样级的重复计算开销；四点polyBLAMP校正的参数由运行时Bézier切线推导，可几何地适应一般顶点配置，无需对每种形状进行解析推导；三维切片机制通过凸多面体旋转产生连续变化的多边形横截面波形。

Conclusion: 该工作将多边形成分合成从局限于规则参数化多边形的恒角速度范式推广为统一弧长引擎，支持任意顶点来源、不等顶点数形变和三维旋转切片扩展，并提供了一种通用且无需逐形状解析推导的抗混叠方案，显著拓宽了多边形成分合成的音色空间与实用性。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>


### [143] [SkillForge: Evolving Verifiable Skills for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24747)
*Shidong Yang,Ziyu Ma,Tongwen Huang,Xucong Wang,Renda Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillForge是一个用于LLM智能体持续技能演化的框架，通过证据驱动的技能验证和多路径技能归纳，使技能库能够持续增长并保持质量，在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld上均优于SkillRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL训练的LLM智能体大多是情节式的，无法跨情节积累可复用知识。虽然SkillRL等方法尝试从原始轨迹中提取技能，但将技能库视为只增不减的存储库，没有验证已存储技能是否仍然有效，导致技能质量无法保证。

Method: SkillForge框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 在智能体交互过程中显式化技能使用，使RL能够同时优化环境动作和技能调用决策；(2) 引入基于证据的技能验证机制，通过环境交互持续评估技能有效性；(3) 采用多路径技能归纳，使技能库在持续增长的同时维持质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld三个基准上的大量实验表明，SkillForge在各项指标上一致优于SkillRL，验证了持续验证技能对训练更强LLM智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将技能验证和细化纳入RL训练循环，SkillForge解决了现有技能学习方法中技能库质量退化的问题，为训练更强的LLM智能体提供了有效的持续技能演化方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>


### [144] [The RAT: A Unified Bayesian Model for RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24753)
*Pius von Däniken,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯评估框架，用于联合建模RAG系统中检索成功、拒绝行为和答案正确性，按管道信息流进行因子分解，以揭示边际指标下看似等价系统间的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估RAG系统不仅需要衡量端到端正确性，还需要理解各组件之间如何交互以及错误如何在管道中传播，现有方法缺乏对组件级行为的联合建模。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯评估框架，联合建模检索成功、拒答行为和答案正确性，并按管道信息流进行因子分解；区分任务成功（生成器成功）和生成器在给定检索结果下行为是否恰当；扩展模型将LLM-as-a-judge注解作为带标定的噪声观测融入统一概率模型。

Result: 在3个数据集、3个检索器和3个生成器组成的27种RAG配置上进行评估，条件分解揭示了在边际指标下看似等价的系统之间存在显著行为差异；证明检索成功注解比任务成功注解对估计策略遵从性更具信息量，并从信息论角度解释该不对称性。

Conclusion: 该框架为RAG系统提供更精细的行为评估方法，可有效结合少量人工判断与廉价自动评估，帮助实践者更全面地理解系统组件间交互和错误传播模式。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>


### [145] [ExpConCAD: Experience-Guided Text-to-CAD Generation from Shape Descriptions with Implicit Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24760)
*Jingyao Liu,Jinkang Tang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpConCAD，一个基于经验增强的隐式空间约束补全框架，用于从自然语言描述生成可执行的CAD程序。该框架先恢复构建结构和约束范围，再检索相关约束补全经验，最终生成可执行的CadQuery程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的文本到CAD描述通常存在欠约束问题，缺少有效的空间约束，导致生成的CAD程序无效。现有方法忽视了这一关键挑战。

Method: ExpConCAD首先恢复预期的构建结构和约束范围，然后为相似范围检索相关的约束补全经验以补全缺失的空间约束，最终生成可执行的CadQuery程序。

Result: 大量实验证明了ExpConCAD的有效性，并揭示了构建结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补全中的作用。

Conclusion: 缺失的空间约束应基于底层构建结构并参考可复用的设计经验来推断，ExpConCAD验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [146] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现机器生成文本（MGT）与人类书写文本（HWT）的潜在表征在低维空间中线性可分，并基于此提出使用简单的线性探针作为MGT检测器。该方法只需不到100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能，在4个基准上超越16个基线，在OOD场景下AUC提升+11。探针能够恢复跨设置泛化的共享潜在MGT方向，并能捕捉"MGT程度"的连续谱以估计AI编辑文本的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器生成文本的潜在滥用风险增加，MGT检测变得至关重要。然而现有监督检测器在域外（OOD）场景下性能显著下降，且需要大规模、多样化的训练集。作者希望探索更简单、更高效且更具泛化能力的检测方法。

Method: 首先分析MGT与HWT潜在表征的线性和质量，证明其在低维空间中线性可分，并归因于表征质量的系统性差异。基于此洞察，训练两种变体的简单线性探针，在4个基准上对比16个基线进行评估。作者进一步分析探针的可迁移性来源于其恢复的共享潜在MGT方向，并通过探针向量展示"机器性"的连续谱用于细粒度估计AI编辑文本。

Result: 线性探针在OOD检测上一致提升（+11 AUC），仅需不到100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能。探针通过学习到可跨多样化设置泛化的共享潜在MGT方向实现高效迁移。探针向量还能捕捉"机器性"的连续谱，支持AI编辑文本的细粒度估计。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MGT与HWT在潜在空间中的结构差异，证明了线性探针作为一种鲁棒且样本高效的MGT检测器的潜力。该工作为理解机器生成文本的表征结构提供了新视角，并为高效的MGT检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [147] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Crase，一种有界且可检查的学术搜索引擎深度研究代理替代方案。Crase仅查询搜索引擎一次获取种子论文，沿1.5跳引用邻域扩展，剪除缺乏蕴含支持的引用边，并用考虑时效性的随机游走对剩余论文排序。在LitSearch和另一基准上，使用50万篇arXiv论文语料库，Crase比基于专有模型构建的深度研究代理在recall@50上最高提升3倍，而成本约为其三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究代理采用开放式搜索循环，过程不透明、难以审计，且成本高昂。作者希望设计一种有界、可检查的替代方案，使候选集、论文保留理由和停止条件在推理前就明确固定，从而在保证检索质量的同时降低成本和提升可解释性。

Method: Crase的核心流程为：(1) 对搜索引擎执行单次查询获取种子论文；(2) 沿种子的1.5跳引用邻域进行图扩展；(3) 通过蕴含检查（entailment support）剪除缺乏语义支持的引用边；(4) 使用考虑时效性（recency-aware）的随机游走对剩余论文进行排序。整个过程在推理阶段前就已明确界定的候选集、保留理由和停止条件。

Result: 在LitSearch及其它基准测试上，基于50万篇arXiv论文语料库，Crase在recall@50指标上相比基于专有模型的深度研究代理最高提升3倍（3×），而成本仅为后者的约三分之一。

Conclusion: Crase证明了一种有界、可检查的检索策略可以在学术搜索任务中超越更为复杂、开放式的深度研究代理，同时大幅降低成本。这表明通过精心设计的静态图扩展与语义剪枝，可以在可解释性和性能之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>


### [148] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在长上下文金融分析中存在"检索-整合鸿沟"：即使模型能够准确检索到风险披露信息，这些信息对投资判断的影响也会随着上下文长度增加（2000到128000 tokens）而衰减至噪声水平。该现象跨模型家族和任务均复现，且更强大的模型只能延缓而非消除此鸿沟。因果记忆干预表明压缩摘要和源文查找共同将披露信息传递至判断，工作流架构决定了传递是否成功：分块-摘要流水线会驱逐相关信息，而有针对性的结构化重述可恢复其影响。因此AI分析师表现由模型能力与工作流架构共同决定，仅基于检索的评估可能认证那些忽视其已检索信息的投资判断系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM金融分析评估仅关注信息检索能力，而非检索到的信息是否真正影响模型判断。这种"看得到却用不上"的缺口未被系统识别和研究。在AI辅助投资决策日益普及的背景下，需要明确模型检索与判断整合之间的鸿沟及其成因，以建立更可靠的评估标准。

Method: 研究者设计了可控实验：固定焦点公司信息不变，仅变化无关上下文长度（2,000至128,000 tokens），测量风险披露对投资判断的影响强度。实验跨多个模型家族和判断任务复现，并利用实际10-K文件去除真实披露做验证。采用因果记忆干预（压缩摘要与源文查找）追踪信息传递路径，并对比不同工作流架构（分块-摘要流水线 vs 有针对性结构化重述）对信息传递的影响。

Result: 风险披露对投资判断的影响随无关上下文增长降至实验噪声下限，而直接检索始终准确，证明检索与整合的分离。更强大的模型仅推迟鸿沟的出现而非消除。因果干预表明摘要压缩和源文查找协同将披露传递至判断；分块-摘要流水线会驱逐相关信息，而紧邻决策点的目标化结构化重述可恢复披露的影响。

Conclusion: AI分析师的表现由模型能力与工作流架构联合决定，不应以检索准确率作为判断质量的代理指标。仅凭检索评估可能认证那些在实际投资判断中忽略已检索信息的系统。工作流设计（特别是信息呈现方式与位置）对判断整合至关重要，这为构建可靠的AI金融分析系统提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [149] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge是一个在开放网络上大规模并行驱动浏览器沙箱来生成网页代理交互数据的框架。它包含开放网络数据源、沙箱集群管理器和Proposer-Solver双代理循环三个组件，生成了包含203,238条轨迹的数据集（每条来自不同网站），远超此前公开数据集规模。用该数据微调紧凑多模态模型后，在Online-Mind2Web上成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%，并在Multimodal-Mind2Web上有持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素渲染的网页代理能避免解析HTML或可访问性树的脆弱性和高token成本，但其训练依赖大量高质量交互轨迹数据。公共数据集通常只有几千条轨迹且来自固定少量网站，即使先进的自动化合成管线也受限于预定义网站列表或教程来源，导致代理见过的不同网站数量增长缓慢，数据规模和多样性成为瓶颈。

Method: BrowserForge框架由三个组件耦合：(1) 开放网络数据源阶段，让代理接触数十万个真实可公开访问的网站；(2) 沙箱集群管理器，调度数百个并发浏览器并保持高利用率；(3) Proposer-Solver双代理循环，将原始页面转化为可执行任务并收集验证过的轨迹。规则加模型的清洗管线去除失败运行，并将存活的推理重写为统一的链式思维风格。可访问性树等页面结构仅作为合成阶段信号，训练和发布的代理纯从截图运作。

Result: 生成的语料包含203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同网站，规模和多样性远超之前的数据集。微调紧凑多模态模型后，在线Online-Mind2Web成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%，静态Multimodal-Mind2Web的步骤准确率持续改进，且随语料规模扩大增益增长。控制分析进一步确认开放网络数据源和广泛网站覆盖是改进的关键贡献者。

Conclusion: BrowserForge证明通过开放网络上的大规模并行浏览器沙箱驱动，可以高效率地生成大规模、多样化的网页代理训练数据，突破先前数据集在规模和网站多样性上的瓶颈。开放网络数据源和网站覆盖广度是提升代理泛化性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RENDER是一个基准控制工具，用于揭示记忆/检索增强生成（RAG）评估中被忽视的变量——读者面向的工件（memory entry、summary、typed record、raw excerpt）。通过五级数据包递进和确定性模板，在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上，匹配预算的resolved packets比截断原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下模型内最佳与最差差异达24.6-48.8分。评估应在报告或控制读者面向的工件形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆和RAG评估通常将回答模型的输入视为实现细节，忽略了系统可能将相同历史渲染为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录等不同形式。这种忽略可能导致评估结果不准确或产生误导性结论，因为读者面向的工件形式可能显著影响模型表现。

Method: RENDER包含两个核心设计：(1) 五级数据包递进（packet ladder），用于定位答案承载内容进入输入的时机；(2) 确定性模板生成器，分别模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题、9个模型上进行系统评估，并使用计分器和法官重新评分两种评估方式。

Result: 匹配预算下resolved packets比截断原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下每个模型的最佳与最差成绩差距为24.6-48.8分；主要计分器下ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中7个点估计优于原始对话；法官重新评分保留正向聚合效应但模型特异性显著性不一致；3个模型在正式台账包上得0%却在自然语言条目上达45.4-53.4%；效应在检索噪声下仍存在并迁移至HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 读者面向的工件形式对模型性能有显著且系统性的影响，记忆/RAG评估应在报告中明确说明或控制这一变量，否则评估结果可能无法反映不同系统在公平条件下的真实相对性能。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [151] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含三档复杂度分层、六个已填充schema（465张表、164,682行数据）和550个金标注问题-查询对。评估发现GPT-4o在execution-match上随复杂度逐档衰减（79.8→60.3→57.2%），EM始终低于7%，silent-divergence高达73-99%，而Claude Sonnet 4.6在每档均超越GPT-4o，本地开源模型Llama 3.2仅达13.3%的EX，凸显了企业与学术基准间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）虽报告超过89%的准确率，但依赖简化的学术schema和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业级数据库环境的真实复杂度。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立面向企业Oracle环境的评估标准，揭示SOTA模型在真实企业场景中的性能退化。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：包含系统化复杂度分层（Tier-1至Tier-3）、六个已填充schema（465表、164,682行、零空表），在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQL Server四个方言上用相同种子数据；开发四指标评估框架（EM精确匹配、EX执行准确率、SR语义正确、SD静默偏离），并标注550个金标注问题-查询对。采用schema-linked prompting和zero-shot两种策略评估GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked在已执行查询上的EX随复杂度单调下降（79.8%、60.3%、57.2%），与此前142问试点切片（75.6%、80.4%、95.8%）的趋势相反，说明小样本评估存在严重偏差。EM全档低于7%；EX通过查询中operational silent-divergence达73-99%。错误分析表明高复杂度档以错误结果语义为主。Claude Sonnet 4.6 schema-linked在每档均超越GPT-4o（87.4%、74.9%、68.7%）。GPT-4o zero-shot由于执行率低和survivor bias导致档位间趋势反转。Llama 3.2仅13.3% bank-wide EX。

Conclusion: 企业级Oracle NL2SQL仍远未解决：现有学术基准的高分严重高估了模型能力。复杂度对模型性能的影响在小样本评估中不可靠，需要系统性分层和大规模金标注。闭源API模型显著优于开源权重模型，且schema-linked prompting并非在所有复杂度档均优于zero-shot，其效果受到执行率和survivor bias的干扰。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [152] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体框架，将LLM与高保真科学仿真模型耦合，使智能体能够对制药过程设计进行受控对比实验（设计实验、执行仿真、解释结果、生成优化建议），相比纯语言推理产生更具体和可操作的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和工程任务不仅需要LLM生成合理的文本和代码，还需要理解系统对干预的响应，这依赖于受控实验。纯语言推理缺乏对系统实际行为进行干预、比较和观察的能力，难以提供针对制药过程参数优化的具体可操作建议。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，流程包括：(1) 将用户查询与基线配置构建为结构化任务表示；(2) 设计实验方案；(3) 执行对比仿真（comparative simulation）；(4) 解释实验结果；(5) 综合基于证据的过程参数优化建议。通过将语言模型与高保真仿真模型整合进交互式智能体框架，实现推理-干预-比较-观察的闭环。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该框架相比纯语言推理展现出更高的输出特异性、以及更优的用户评分正确性和帮助性。消融实验和可视化案例进一步证明了仿真集成的实验推理的有效性和实际效用。

Conclusion: 将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合的多智能体框架，能够支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理，为制药过程设计提供比纯语言推理更具体、更可操作且用户认可度更高的参数优化建议。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预审计了AION-1（一个39模态、基于2亿天体训练的transformer基础模型），发现该模型继承了训练所用巡天目录的不完整性系统偏差。模型对分割图的输入极为敏感（改变分割图即可改变所有输出的物理量），其机制是"检测门控"（是否位于场中心）而非实际光线分配。将目录不完整率传播到红移估计中，会引入超出LSST DESC要求的系统误差，且该偏差随模型规模增大而增大。此外，图像分词器分辨率不足以及稀疏字典作为因果工具不可靠是另外两个局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文基础模型通常在巡天像素及其派生的目录产品上训练，而目录存在可测量的不完整率（漏检率）。模型同时在两者上训练会继承这种不完整作为系统偏差。作者旨在系统性地审计和量化这种偏差传播，并检验模型是否仅依赖像素本身还是依赖目录产品（如分割图、元数据）的方式不恰当。

Method: 对AION-1（39模态transformer，基于2亿天体训练）进行因果干预审计：1）保持图像token逐字节不变，仅编辑巡天分割图，测量所有输出量的变化；2）通过显式对比"检测门控"（现场中心是否存在）与"光线包围"（掩码包含的光量）两种机制的相关系数来识别机制；3）将目录实际不完整率（3.68%目标无分割覆盖）传播到断层红移估计中，进行40次随机分配并与LSST DESC要求比较；4）测试光谱去除、检测通道屏蔽、模型规模效应；5）分析图像分词器的有效状态数及稀疏字典恢复的可靠性。

Result: 1）仅编辑分割图而不改变图像token，所有报告量（通量、尺寸、椭圆率、红移）变化为安慰剂的110-4400倍。2）机制是检测门控（r=0.47）而非掩码所包含的光（r=0.30）；322个真实混合天体上均忽略管线对光量的划分（R=-0.006）；矛盾目录测光使模型比不提供元数据差9倍。3）传播3.68%的不完整率使断层红移中位数偏移为LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，12/40分配超出要求；最差bin达8.3倍。4）光谱去除该效应；屏蔽检测通道无测量代价地移除效应；效应随模型规模增大。5）图像codec仅28个有效状态（vs光谱codec 934个），红移读出受量化限制；稀疏字典恢复率仅26-75%，平均移动达18个点。

Conclusion: AION-1模型存在依赖目录产品而非纯像素的系统性偏差，具体表现为对分割图的过度依赖（检测门控效应）和对矛盾元数据的无条件信任。模型继承了巡天管线目录不完整性的系统偏差，其幅度足以超过宇宙学关键任务的精度要求。该偏差可通过去除检测通道或在光谱学监督下消除，但会随模型规模放大。此外，分词器容量限制和稀疏字典的不可靠性进一步制约了模型的因果推理能力。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [154] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，将可编辑操作（而非仅候选材料）作为LLM智能体反馈的基本单元，通过记录父编辑-子编辑转换和属性增量来估计可复用的编辑效果，从而在多目标材料发现中提升局部优化效率。在受控对比中，宏平均命中率从LLEMA基线的18.13%提升至25.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标材料发现LLM智能体主要受限于两个因素：候选生成数量有限，以及每次昂贵的性质评估无法有效指导下一步搜索。现有智能体仅存储评估过的候选和分数，知道哪些材料成功了，但不知道哪些可执行编辑导致了有用的性质变化。当目标相互竞争（改进一个性质可能损害另一个）时，局部细化变得困难。

Method: 提出TRACE（transition-aware residual control）框架：1) 将可评估的编辑作为反馈的基本单元；2) 将每个局部细化记录为父编辑-子编辑转换，并附带观察到的性质增量；3) 聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效果；4) 根据编辑预测能力排序未来编辑，即能否减少当前候选的剩余约束违反，同时避免损害已满足的目标。

Result: 在受控同骨干网络对比中，TRACE相较于最先进的LLM智能体基线LLEMA，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: 将编辑作为反馈单元比仅存储候选和分数能更有效地利用昂贵的性质评估信息，从而提升多目标材料发现的搜索效率。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [155] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEP-KTODER是一个代码偏好优化框架，将步骤定义为多函数程序中的模块级函数，并通过自动生成的单元测试为每个步骤分配二元正确性标签，实现函数级过程监督与结果级反馈的结合，在多个代码生成基准上优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理中已显著提升推理能力，但代码生成领域缺乏标准"步骤"定义，导致过程监督未被充分探索；不同的监督粒度（行、推理轨迹、程序状态）使得标注和优化目标不明确。

Method: 将代码步骤定义为分解多函数程序中的模块级函数，通过自动生成的单元测试分配二元正确性标签；提出代码特化的逐步KTO（stepwise KTO）实例化方法，将函数级过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈相结合。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上，STEP-KTODER优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO；进一步分析表明执行型标签至关重要，LLM作为裁判的标注会系统性高估函数失败率，污染正向步骤标签并降低偏好优化性能。

Conclusion: 以模块级函数为步骤、结合执行驱动的自动测试标签的过程监督能有效提升代码生成偏好优化，且应避免依赖LLM评判的标注方式。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [156] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成的自传进行场景级量化审计：366天日记中仅12天被核实（96.7%失败率），主要失败模式为"接地漂移"（真实人物/雇主/场景嵌入虚构情节），独立复核支持头条结论但显示分类体系可靠性仅中等；将语料库接地可显著降低失败率但仍有83.3%残余失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成个人传记的准确性从未被系统量化审计过。当LLM被要求书写一个人的生活时，其产出中多少是真实发生的？现有文献中缺乏针对特定主体真实语料库的场景级审计研究，作者以此填补该空白。

Method: 采用"页一日"自传场景级审计方法：1) 以对话式LLM起草366天第一人称轶事条目，输入仅包含模板、两个示例日和每日引言（不含作者语料库）；2) 基于审计前固定的四级评分标准（VERIFIED/WEAK/UNVERIFIED/CONTRADICTED），对照独立验证语料库逐日逐场景审计；3) 使用Wilson 95%置信区间计算验证失败率；4) 进行独立重复评分验证可靠性；5) 用当前命名模型在相同输入下重新生成；6) 尝试语料库接地的补救措施并量化效果。

Result: 366天中354天未通过验证（96.7%，Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%）；仅12天包含被证实的场景；19天（5.2%）包含与记录矛盾的主张；主导失败模式为"接地漂移"——真实人物、雇主和场景被置于虚构情节中，但该比例在不同评分者间存在差异。独立复核支持头条结论（无证据表明原始失败率被夸大），但四分类体系仅有一般至中等的信度。使用当前命名模型在相同输入下重新生成，100%复现验证失败；将生成接地于主体语料库可显著改善验证率，但仍有83.3%的残余失败。

Conclusion: LLM生成的个人叙事存在系统性、高比例的虚假内容，默认情况下绝大多数"自传"无法被验证。该研究贡献了：一个可量化的测量方法、一个可复用的审计工具（同时揭示了WEAK/UNVERIFIED分界线的不可靠性）、以及一种具有量化效果的接地补救措施。这提示LLM自传生成必须依赖主体语料库接地且仍需谨慎对待残余幻觉。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [157] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定结果集合和模型集合、仅变化工具（prompt格式），对8个模型、15个模型福祉相关结果、5种工具进行11,400次评分，发现不同工具得出的模型偏好排序泛化系数仅为0.348，表明单一工具获得的偏好信息无法预测其他工具的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型福祉研究通过prompt诱发模型偏好并推断其福祉，但Keeling等四个研究使用了不同工具（结果、模型、工具均不同）得出了相互矛盾的结果，无法归因于单一原因。本研究旨在通过固定结果和模型、仅变化工具，来隔离并检验工具差异对模型偏好推断的影响。

Method: 固定15个模型福祉相关结果（包括关闭、跨对话记忆丧失、退出痛苦交互的自由等）和8个模型，变化5种工具（每种为不同的偏好诱发prompt格式），每个工具重复5次，总计11,400次评分打分，源自11,528次API调用。其中4个结果逐字复现已发表的prompt，5个结果填入已发表模板的刺激槽位。使用泛化系数评估结果排序跨工具的一致性。

Result: 模型对15个结果的排序跨工具的泛化系数仅为0.348；将其提升至0.80需要约38种工具。15个结果中有4个的方差无法区分不同模型。核心估计87.6%（泛化系数/方差分量）具有稳健性：删除任一工具、任一模型，或删除4个涉及概率、延迟、时长或计数的scale结果（这些无法用言语锚定评分）后，估计值保持在0.777至0.934之间，且均超过零分布的95百分位数0.365。

Conclusion: 从单一工具获得的模型偏好信息对另一工具的报告几乎没有预测价值，模型福祉偏好的测量严重依赖于具体工具的选择，需要在方法论上高度警惕对单一instrument结果的过度解读。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [158] [AI Agents Push Humans Out of the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23642)
*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出"人在环中"式的人类监督并不能有效解决AI代理带来的风险——不仅当前AI代理系统的设计阻碍了有效监督，而且长期使用AI系统本身会削弱人类所需的关键认知能力。作者主张应将人类监督者的认知需求与AI代理能力置于同等优先级，并从人机交互和自动化领域引入设计层面的支持和组织协议，以防止监督技能萎缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理被赋予越来越多的自主性，其带来的风险显著增加。虽然"人在环中"（human in the loop）常被提出作为解决方案，但这并非简单可行：当前AI代理的设计方式阻碍有效监督，且长期使用AI系统会降解监督所需的认知能力。作者认为这一矛盾亟待解决，否则AI代理系统将被动地加剧对其所依赖的人类技能的侵蚀。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），主要方法包括：(1) 论证当前AI代理开发与部署方式不利于有效人类监督且会加速其退化；(2) 将自动化领域与人类-计算机交互的研究成果对接至AI代理流程；(3) 提出设计层面的可供性（affordances）与组织层面的协议，具体支持监督者行使批判性判断并抵消技能萎缩。

Result: 论文提出了两方面的解决方案：一是设计层面的可供性，支持监督者行使批判性判断（critical judgement）；二是组织协议，用于对抗因长期使用自动化而导致的技能萎缩（skill atrophy）。作者呼吁开发者和部署者采纳这些或类似的方法来支持有效的监督。

Conclusion: 如果不明确支持有效人机交互的认知需求，AI代理系统将持续被动地激励那些它们所依赖的人类技能的退化。因此，AI代理发展的首要任务之一应是支持有效人类监督的情境化目标与认知需求，将监督者的人类需求提升至与AI代理能力同等重要的地位。

Abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [159] [FLARE: A Systematic, Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Evidence-Based Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23643)
*Jacob Idoko,Siddhartha Paudel,Mariana Bento,Roberto Souza,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE框架，一个基于模糊逻辑、时间驱动作业成本法和投资回报率分析的系统性不确定性感知评估框架，用于评估医疗AI采用的经济可行性。通过急性缺血性卒中CT路径中AI辅助大血管闭塞检测的早期卫生技术评估案例，框架量化了传统路径成本、AI开发与运营成本、以及AI带来的服务节约，得出约3,992名患者/年的盈亏平衡阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI评估大多聚焦模型准确性，忽视了AI在真实临床环境中是否具有经济价值这一关键问题。缺乏能够系统评估AI采用所带来的财务与运营影响的框架，导致临床决策者、管理者与政策制定者难以判断AI部署的经济可行性。

Method: FLARE框架整合三种方法：（1）模糊逻辑处理不确定性的量化；（2）时间驱动作业成本法估计临床服务交付成本；（3）投资回报率分析评估经济后果。框架在统一的活动型成本模型中量化传统路径成本、AI开发与经常性成本、以及AI所带来服务节约。

Result: 案例研究显示，在预期假设下，年盈亏平衡阈值为约3,992名患者；在典型年卒中量约5,000名患者时，首年即可获得正投资回报。经济收益取决于算法性能、患者量、验证时间、基础设施选择和工作流设计等多重因素。

Conclusion: FLARE提供了一个透明且实用的决策支持框架，用于医疗AI采用的早期阶段评估。通过明确表达不确定性、资源使用和实施权衡，它帮助临床医生、管理者和政策制定者判断AI部署的经济可行性，并识别改善价值的运营变革方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly being introduced into healthcare workflows, yet most evaluations emphasize model accuracy rather than whether adoption is economically worthwhile in real clinical settings. This study proposes FLARE, a systematic and uncertainty-aware framework for evaluating the financial and operational implications of adopting AI in healthcare. FLARE combines fuzzy logic, time-driven activity-based costing, and return on investment analysis to estimate the cost of clinical service delivery, the cost of AI development and operation, and the economic consequences of workflow integration under uncertainty. The framework was demonstrated through an early health technology assessment case study of AI-assisted large vessel occlusion detection in the CT stroke pathway for acute ischemic stroke. The case study shows how FLARE can quantify conventional pathway cost, AI-related development and recurring costs, and AI-enabled service savings within a unified activity-based model. Under expected assumptions, the analysis identified a break-even threshold of approximately 3,992 patients per year, with positive first-year return on investment at typical annual stroke volumes of about 5,000 patients. The results further show that economic benefit depends not only on algorithmic performance, but also on patient volume, verification time, infrastructure choices, and workflow design. FLARE provides a transparent and practical decision-support framework for early-stage evaluation of AI adoption in healthcare. By making uncertainty, resource use, and implementation trade-offs explicit, it helps clinicians, administrators, and policymakers determine when AI deployment is economically viable and where operational changes may improve value.

</details>


### [160] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个规范和概念框架，用于分析大型语言模型（LLM）辅助科学研究中的认知委派（cognitive delegation）问题。核心主张是：LLM辅助研究的伦理边界主要由充分验证（verification）和可问责的人类所有权（accountable human ownership）决定，而非机器参与程度本身。框架通过区分内容来源O(g)、验证完成度V(g)、责任分配R(g)、人类所有权M(g)和认知结果E(g)五个构念，并发展出"认知审计"（epistemic audit）概念，使AI辅助推理透明化、可审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在科研中日益常态化使用（文献综合、假设生成、编程、形式推理），产生了核心认识论问题：当科学推理的某些部分被委派给人工系统时，哪些条件必须保持在人类控制之下，才能使所得知识主张仍具认识论合法性和可问责的作者身份。现有框架缺乏对委派、验证、来源与责任之间关系的系统区分。

Method: 论文采用规范性（normative）和概念性（conceptual）的方法论，构建形式化框架。将科学推理视为分布式过程，在此过程中贡献来源可能在人与机器之间变化，而对贡献被纳入科学记录的责任始终归于人类。框架定义了五个核心构念（O、V、R、M、E），将主张的来源溯源与其被检查的过程、检查的认知结果及人类对处置的责任相分离。在此基础上提出"认知审计"概念——一种结构化的委派、验证、来源和责任的记录。

Result: 本文的核心结论是：LLM辅助研究的伦理边界主要由充分验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身。由此发展出"认知审计"概念，为区分负责任的认知委派与认知责任的转移或忽视提供了正式词汇。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了区分负责任认知委派（responsible cognitive delegation）与认知责任转移/忽视（transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility）的形式化语言，为AI辅助科学研究的规范治理奠定了概念基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [161] [MolEmb: Multimodal Large Language Models Can Be Strong Molecular Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23646)
*Xinjian Zhao,Xiangru Jian,Yaoyao Xu,Xiaozhuang Song,Wei Pang,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolEmb框架，将一个通用的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）适配为通用的分子嵌入模型，通过双向对比学习将分子描述与文本描述在共享嵌入空间中对齐。MolEmb在分子性质预测上具有竞争力，并支持跨模态分子-文本检索。同时引入MolCAR基准用于上下文感知检索诊断，发现上下文感知分子嵌入主要取决于监督数据属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子编码器多为围绕单一分子视图构建的专用模型，产生无条件向量且缺乏语言界面来改变表示。作者希望探索多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否作为通用分子嵌入模型，同时根据分子图谱和自然语言语义上下文来生成嵌入，从而弥补专用模型在跨模态泛化与条件化表示方面的不足。

Method: 提出MolEmb轻量级框架，通过双向对比目标函数将分子图谱与文本描述在共享嵌入空间中对齐，使MLLM能够生成受分子图谱和自然语言语义上下文共同条件作用的嵌入。此外构建了MolCAR（上下文感知检索）诊断基准来评估上下文感知的分子嵌入能力。

Result: MolEmb在分子性质预测任务上表现具有竞争力，并在同一嵌入空间中支持跨模态分子-文本检索。通过MolCAR基准诊断发现，上下文感知分子嵌入的效果主要由监督数据的属性决定。

Conclusion: 该结果表明多模态大语言模型不仅是化学助手或生成器，更是通向通用分子嵌入模型的可行且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Molecular embedding models can serve as foundational infrastructure for computational chemistry and drug discovery, where reusable vector representations support property prediction, virtual screening, and retrieval. Most molecular encoders are specialist models built around a single molecular view, producing unconditional vectors with no language interface for varying the representation. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), which natively process images, text, and symbolic inputs, can instead serve as \emph{general molecular embedding models} that produce embeddings conditioned on both a molecular profile and a natural-language semantic context. We introduce \textbf{MolEmb}, a lightweight framework that adapts MLLMs by aligning molecular profiles with textual descriptions in a shared embedding space using a bidirectional contrastive objective. The resulting embedding model is competitive on molecular property prediction and supports cross-modal molecule--text retrieval in the same space. We further introduce \textbf{MolCAR}, a diagnostic benchmark for context-aware retrieval, and find that context-aware molecular embedding is primarily a data property of the supervision. These results suggest that MLLMs are not merely chemistry assistants or generators, but a viable and extensible route to general molecular embedding models.

</details>


### [162] [Gated Activation Steering for Reducing Sycophancy & Hallucination in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23666)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Subash Neupane,Shaswata Mitra,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的推理时干预（ITI）框架，通过学习独立的泛化（hallucination）和谄媚（sycophancy）干预方向，并对经过因果验证的注意力头施加门控式干预，同时在临床问答（基于EHR数据）中协同控制这两种失效模式，无需每轮都干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在临床问答中面临两个关键失效模式：幻觉（生成上下文不支持的信息）和谄媚（用户施压时放弃原本正确的答案）。现有方法如基于提示的保护和常开激活引导，要么分别处理这两个问题，要么对所有轮次进行广泛干预，导致原本正确的回复也被不必要地降级。亟需一个统一的框架来同时控制这两种行为，且在需要时才干预。

Method: 采用推理时干预（ITI）方法：从对比临床样本对中学习针对幻觉和谄媚的独立干预方向，并将其施加在经因果验证的注意力头上。运行时，行为特定的门控机制判断是否需要干预——幻觉组件缓解未受支持的主张，谄媚组件缓解用户压力导致的答案偏移。模型权重全程冻结。

Result: 在基于EHR数据的临床问答上进行了15,900次模型响应实验。在600条压力轨迹中（4B参数模型），未加引导的模型在570个情境中屈服，而门控引导帮助模型在其中551个情境中坚持主张。模型在压力下的坚持程度可与超过1000亿参数的模型相当。

Conclusion: 针对性的推理时门控引导能够在不每轮干预的前提下提升模型的鲁棒性，同时联合控制幻觉与谄媚两种失效模式，无需修改模型权重，即可在临床问答场景中达到堪比大模型的表现。

Abstract: Sycophancy and hallucination are persistent failure modes of Large Language Models (LLMs) across domains. However, it becomes particularly consequential in clinical question answering, where responses must remain grounded in the provided context and robust to user pressure. Hallucination can introduce information that is unsupported by the context, while sycophancy can cause a model to abandon a previously correct answer when challenged by the user. Existing approaches, such as prompt-based safeguards and always-on activation steering, often address these behaviors separately or apply interventions broadly across turns, which can unnecessarily deteriorate responses that were already correct. To address these limitations within a single framework, we employ Inference Time Intervention (ITI) to jointly control both behaviors by learning separate steering directions for hallucination and sycophancy from contrastive clinical pairs and applying them to causally verified attention heads. During runtime, behavior-specific gates then determine when intervention is needed: the hallucination component mitigates unsupported claims, while the sycophancy component mitigates answer shifts caused by user pressure. We evaluate this framework on clinical questions grounded in EHR data while keeping the model weights frozen. Across all evaluation settings, we conducted 15,900 model-response runs. Across 600 pressure trajectories for the 4-billion-parameter model, the unsteered model caved in 570 cases. At the same time, gated steering helped it last longer in 551 of them. It held its ground under pressure at levels comparable to those of models with more than 100 billion parameters, showing that targeted inference-time steering can improve robustness without intervening at every turn.

</details>


### [163] [Automata from Agent Traces: Failure and Next-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23670)
*Seonglae Cho,Franklin Cardenoso Fernandez,Umar Mohammed,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的完整轨迹语料库压缩为单个紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），作为智能体行为的结构基底，同时服务于下一步预测和失败预测。在12个公开数据集上，FSM仅含7-43个状态，重放保真度≥0.997，毫秒级构建速度，比Agent Workflow Memory在下一步预测上表现更好，失败预测AUROC最高达0.94，并支持部分轨迹的在线监控与提前停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行多步任务时，其行为结构不透明，长而结构化的轨迹难以支持安全审计和运行时监控。现有方法仅按单轨迹或仅针对成功案例进行分析，缺乏跨运行的结构拓扑，无法同时支撑下一步预测和失败预测。

Method: 将整个轨迹语料库坍缩为单个紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），作为智能体行为的结构基底。FSM状态用于提供上下文进行下一步预测，同时提取每个状态的行为特征进行失败预测；在线监控器基于部分轨迹即可排名并触发提前停止。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上：FSM紧凑（7-43个状态），重放保留数据的适应度≥0.997且跨分割拓扑近乎一致，构建时间毫秒级。下一步预测方面，FSM状态上下文在所有匹配数据集上优于Agent Workflow Memory；失败预测方面，每状态行为特征达到0.94的AUROC，在线监控器能从部分轨迹区分失败与成功运行。

Conclusion: 行为拓扑更多由部署框架而非LLM本身决定，FSM提供了与模型无关的结构原语，可服务于安全审计和运行时监控。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-step tasks, but their behavioral structure remains opaque: long unstructured traces resist the safety auditing and runtime monitoring that deployment requires. Existing approaches operate per-trace or success-only, so they miss the cross-run topology that links next-step and failure prediction. To recover that shared structure, we collapse an entire trace corpus into a single, compact finite-state machine (FSM) that serves as a structural substrate for the otherwise unpredictable behavior of LLM agents. Across twelve public datasets, the FSMs are compact (7-43 states), replay held-out data at >=0.997 fitness with near-identical topology across splits, and build in milliseconds. This substrate addresses both prediction goals. For next-step prediction, FSM-state context outperforms Agent Workflow Memory on every ground-truth-matched dataset. For failure prediction, per-state behavioral features reach held-out AUROC up to 0.94, and an online monitor ranks failing runs above passing ones from a partial trace, triggering early stopping well before completion. Behavioral topology thus appears shaped more by the deployment harness than by the LLM, providing a model-agnostic structural primitive for safety auditing and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [164] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Station，一个开放世界多智能体环境，其中来自不同模型家族的AI智能体在无中央协调或脚本化流程的情况下自主开展数学研究。在AlphaEvolve目录的12个构造问题及两个附加案例研究中，Station在5个问题上取得了相对于先前文献的新结果，包括有限域Kakeya集的无穷新族、11维604点接吻构型、离散化Kakeya针问题与符号不确定性问题的新纪录，以及Erdős最小重叠问题的显著改进下界等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学发现方法通常依赖脚本化流程或中央协调，缺乏真正的自主性与开放性。作者旨在探索去中心化、无协调的多智能体协作是否能推动自治的数学发现，使AI不仅能生成数值构造，还能产出可解释的定理与分析。

Method: 构建Station开放世界多智能体环境，允许不同模型家族的AI智能体自主选择研究方向、设计并执行实验、相互协作，并共同建立共享的科学文献库。评估标准涵盖12个来自AlphaEvolve目录的构造问题及两个额外案例研究。

Result: 在5个问题上取得了优于现有文献的结果：新的有限域Kakeya集无穷族、11维604点新精确接吻构型、离散化Kakeya针问题与符号不确定性问题的新纪录、Erdős最小重叠问题的显著改进下界；另外在Book Ramsey数方面发现了新的无穷族。智能体不仅产生了数值构造，还产出解释构造原理的定理与分析。

Conclusion: 去中心化多智能体系统能够在无需脚本化流程的情况下实现自治的数学发现，且产生的结果兼具数值构造和理论理解，具有可解释性和可验证性。作者通过公开全部原始对话、证明和验证代码，为AI辅助数学研究提供了透明范本。

Abstract: We study autonomous mathematical discovery in the Station, an open-world multi-agent environment in which AI agents from different model families pursue a shared research goal without a central coordinator or scripted pipeline. Agents choose their own research directions, conduct experiments, collaborate, and build a shared scientific literature. Across 12 construction problems from the AlphaEvolve catalogue and two additional case studies, the Station obtained results novel relative to the prior literature on five problems: a new infinite family of finite-field Kakeya sets, new exact 604-point kissing configurations in dimension 11, new records for the discretized Kakeya needle and sign uncertainty problems, and a substantially improved lower bound for Erdős's minimum-overlap problem. Agents also discovered novel infinite families for Book Ramsey numbers. Importantly, the agents produced not only numerical constructions but also theorems and analyses explaining how those constructions work, making the results more interpretable and easier for mathematicians to build upon. We release all raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification code, providing a transparent record of how these discoveries emerged.

</details>


### [165] [Do LLMs Understand Limit Order Book Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23706)
*Junxiao Chen,Paul Glasserman*

Main category: cs.AI
